Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on p-adic structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 529. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 779 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_529(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 529. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^779(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the p-adic object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 793. The moduli space has dimension at most 594 with 5 connected components.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to p-adic theory, we decompose the problem into 793 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 594. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
